Furthermore, it drastically improves adaptability by allowing new information to be integrated via simple prompt modifications, while enhancing interpretability through natural language reasoning traces. These advances in cross-dataset image classification pave the way for more reliable general-purpose vision systems such as AI assistants and autonomous robots.

</details>


### [82] [MapRoute++: Surrogate-Guided Semantic Routing for Visual Concept Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13478)
*Ashok Urlana,L. D. M. S. Sai Teja,Vivek Hruday Kavuri,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Genμ 2.0挑战赛Task 3（视觉概念遗忘）的参赛方案。基于MapRoute框架，引入任务特定训练目标、更丰富的概念表征和语义路由机制，实现概念特异的映射器选择。在Stable Diffusion v1.4上使用ERR指标评估，平均性能比现有最优基线提升12.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉概念遗忘（concept unlearning）是生成模型安全领域的重要问题——需要从预训练扩散模型中移除特定概念（如受版权保护或有害内容），同时保留不相关及语义相邻的概念不受影响。现有方法的鲁棒性和保留性能存在瓶颈，需要更有效的方案。

Method: 基于MapRoute框架进行三项改进：(1) 引入任务特定（task-specific）的训练目标；(2) 构建更丰富的概念表征（richer concept representations）；(3) 提出语义路由（semantic routing）机制，用于概念特异的映射器（mapper）选择，实现对不同概念分别处理。

Result: 在Genμ 2.0官方基准上，使用Erasing-Retention-Robustness (ERR)指标在Stable Diffusion v1.4上评估，该方法在五个概念类别上平均比现有最先进基线提升12.1%，实现了显著增益。

Conclusion: 通过任务特定训练目标、丰富概念表征和语义路由的结合，该方法在概念遗忘任务上显著优于现有基线，在确保鲁棒移除目标概念的同时，有效保持了不相关和语义邻近概念的完整性，验证了将语义路由与概念特异的映射器选择相结合的有效性。

Abstract: We present our submission to Task 3 of the Gen$μ$ 2.0 Challenge on visual concept unlearning. Building on MapRoute, we introduce task-specific training objectives, richer concept representations, and semantic routing for concept-specific mapper selection. Our approach improves robust concept removal while preserving unrelated and semantically adjacent concepts. On the official benchmark, evaluated using the Erasing-Retention-Robustness (ERR) metric on Stable Diffusion v1.4, our method outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline by 12.1\% on average across the five concept categories, achieving substantial gains.

</details>


### [83] [DreamX-Phi 1.0: Action-Conditioned Video World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13489)
*DreamX Team,Rui Chen,Xiangxiang Chu,Geng Li,Jifan Li,Qingfeng Shi,Datao Tang,Jing Tang,Jun Wang,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamX-Phi 1.0是一个面向机器人操作的动作条件视频世界模型，通过在注意力中注入SE(3)几何编码、深度分支和SAM3+V-JEPA对象一致性机制，以及分布匹配蒸馏，在WorldArena 2.0挑战赛Track 1和Track 2分别取得第一和第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型虽然能生成逼真的滚动视频，但逼真度不等于忠实度——模型可能移动错误的机械臂或丢失被操作的对象。需要确保预测结果严格遵循每条机械臂的命令路径，同时保持场景几何和小型操作对象的连贯性。

Method: 1) 在注意力机制中注入每臂SE(3)变换，采用PRoPE风格的几何编码，保持臂身份和刚体运动结构；2) 添加轻量级深度分支处理场景级几何；3) 使用SAM3掩码配合冻结的V-JEPA教师模型维持抓取过程中的对象一致性；4) 通过分布匹配蒸馏将多步生成器压缩为少步学生模型以提高部署效率。

Result: 在WorldArena 2.0挑战赛中，DreamX-Phi 1.0取得Track 1第一名和Track 2第二名。

Conclusion: 通过结合动作条件几何编码、深度分支和基于掩码-教师的对象一致性机制，可以显著提升视频世界模型在真实度与物理忠实度两个维度上的表现，同时利用蒸馏实现高效部署。

Abstract: We present \textbf{DreamX-Phi 1.0}, an action-conditioned video world model for robotic manipulation that, given an observed frame, a language instruction, and a prescribed action sequence comprising end-effector poses and gripper states, predicts the resulting future observations. Yet realism alone does not guarantee faithfulness: a convincing rollout can still move the wrong arm or lose the manipulated object. To ensure the prediction respects each arm's commanded path, we inject per-arm $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ transformations into attention via \textbf{PRoPE-style geometric encoding}, preserving arm identity and rigid-motion structure. Action control alone does not fully constrain scene geometry or the evolution of small manipulated objects. We therefore add a lightweight \textbf{depth branch} for scene-level geometry and use \textbf{SAM3 masks} with a frozen \textbf{V-JEPA teacher} to maintain object consistency throughout grasping. We further distill the multi-step generator into a few-step student via distribution-matching distillation for efficient deployment. At the time of writing, \model{} achieves first place on Track~1 and second place on Track~2 of the WorldArena~2.0 Challenge. Our model and code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [84] [TraVEL: Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning for Driving-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13495)
*Yi-Chung Chen,Philip Jacobson,Tom Lampo,Yiren Lu,Jin Yao,David I. Inouye,Jing Gao,Danhua Guo,Burhan Yaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TraVEL框架，通过将自车轨迹相似度作为组相对策略优化（GRPO）中的奖励信号，对通用多模态嵌入模型进行运动感知微调，从而提升驾驶视频检索中基于运动事件（如左转/右转、加速/减速）的细粒度理解能力，仅使用单向量视频嵌入即可完成检索，无需专家规则或辅助感知输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模驾驶日志中的高效视频检索对数据整理、模型开发和安全分析至关重要。传统的结构化/规则检索系统虽然能精确匹配驾驶事件，但依赖专家规则、辅助数据和多阶段感知流水线；通用多模态嵌入模型虽简单高效，但往往依赖静态场景上下文捷径，难以区分运动中心事件（如左转vs右转、加速vs减速）。因此需要一种既能理解细粒度运动语义又能保持端到端检索简单性的方法。

Method: 提出TraVEL（Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning）运动感知微调框架。具体包含两步：(1) 首先使用InfoNCE目标在nuReasoning数据集上对配对片段和推理轨迹微调Qwen3-VL-Embedding，提升整体检索性能；(2) 然后引入TraVEL，将自车轨迹相似度作为奖励信号，在组相对策略优化（GRPO）框架中进行运动感知微调。轨迹仅作为训练阶段的特权监督信息，推理时仍只使用单向量视频嵌入，无需自车位姿、专家规则或辅助感知输出。此外构建了基于nuReasoning的驾驶视频检索基准。

Result: 在驾驶视频检索基准上的实验表明，TraVEL在多个模型规模上都显著提升了运动中心检索性能：相比SFT，在2B模型上纵向和横向mAP分别提升9.8和4.7个百分点，在8B模型上分别提升7.2和1.5个百分点。

Conclusion: TraVEL将物理grounded的轨迹监督与高效的嵌入检索相结合，证明轨迹作为特权监督信号能有效弥补字幕监督在细粒度运动理解上的不足，同时保持推理时的简单单向量检索范式。

Abstract: Efficiently retrieving relevant clips from large-scale driving logs is essential for data curation, model development, and safety analysis. Structured and rule-based retrieval systems can explicitly target driving events, but typically require expert-defined rules, auxiliary data, and multi-stage perception pipelines. Multimodal embedding models offer a simpler and more efficient alternative by representing each video with a single searchable vector. However, general-purpose models often rely on shortcuts from static scene context and struggle to distinguish motion-centric events, such as turning left versus right or accelerating versus decelerating. In this work, we study how to adapt a general-purpose multimodal embedding model to driving-video retrieval. We first fine-tune Qwen3-VL-Embedding on paired clips and reasoning traces from nuReasoning using an InfoNCE objective. While this stage substantially improves overall retrieval, caption supervision alone remains insufficient for fine-grained motion understanding. We therefore introduce TraVEL (Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning), a motion-aware fine-tuning framework that uses ego-trajectory similarity as a reward within Group Relative Policy Optimization. Trajectories serve only as privileged training supervision; retrieval still operates on single-vector video embeddings without ego poses, expert rules, or auxiliary perception outputs. We further construct a driving-video retrieval benchmark from nuReasoning. Experiments show that TraVEL improves motion-centric retrieval across model scales: relative to SFT, it raises longitudinal and lateral mAP by 9.8 and 4.7 points at 2B, with corresponding gains of 7.2 and 1.5 points at 8B. TraVEL thus combines physically grounded supervision with efficient embedding-based search.

</details>


### [85] [GS$^{2}$CI: Robust Gaussian Splatting For Snapshot Compressive Imaging via Large Vision Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13502)
*Yanming Yang,Chenxi Song,Ping Wang,Xin Yuan,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个新颖框架，从单次快照压缩成像（SCI）测量中利用3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）和大规模视觉基础模型（VFM）的先验知识重建高质量3D场景。方法结合测量导出的3D VFM初始化和SCI感知的高斯优化，辅以2D VFM提供的伪视角监督进行外观细化，并提出SCI特定的致密化策略（OSGR）来解决监督模糊导致的不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SCI用于3D场景重建的方法面临三大挑战：信息丢失导致的重建质量低下、有限的视点多样性、以及联合优化3D表示与相机位姿的沉重计算负担。需要一种能从单一SCI测量中高效重建高质量3D场景的方法。

Method: 方法由三部分组成：（1）主重建阶段：利用测量导出的3D VFM初始化3DGS，并结合SCI感知的高斯优化；（2）辅助细化阶段：粗阶段收敛后，使用2D VFM在合成视点提供伪视角监督，用于局部外观细化；（3）OSGR致密化策略：包括基于局部不透明度统计扩展分裂候选、通过均值不透明度正则化抑制损失补偿性的不透明度膨胀、以及通过显式候选比例和高斯数量约束来控制表示增长。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在整体性能上表现最佳，结合了领先的重建质量、对视角变化的鲁棒性以及有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 该方法通过融合3DGS与VFM先验，有效克服了SCI重建3D场景中的信息损失和优化不稳定问题，实现了高质量且计算高效的单次测量3D场景重建，并验证了OSGR致密化策略在SCI特定监督下的有效性。

Abstract: Snapshot Compressive Imaging (SCI) offers an efficient solution for high-speed video acquisition and, under exposure-time camera--scene relative motion, multi-view scene capture by compressing temporal or spatial information into a single 2D measurement. While recent studies have explored SCI for 3D scene reconstruction, existing methods struggle with significant challenges due to information loss, limited viewpoint diversity, and the computational burden of jointly optimizing 3D representations and camera poses. In this work, we propose a novel framework that reconstructs high-quality 3D scenes from a single SCI measurement by leveraging 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and the powerful priors of large-scale vision foundation models (VFMs). Our primary reconstruction combines measurement-derived 3D VFM initialization with SCI-aware Gaussian optimization. After coarse-stage convergence, an auxiliary 2D VFM provides pseudo-view supervision at synthesized viewpoints for local appearance refinement. To further address the instability caused by ambiguous SCI supervision during 3DGS optimization, we introduce Opacity-Guided Splitting and Growth Regulation (OSGR), an SCI-specific densification strategy that augments split candidates using local opacity statistics, discourages loss-compensating opacity inflation through mean-opacity regulation, and bounds representation growth with explicit candidate-ratio and Gaussian-count constraints. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves the strongest overall performance, combining leading reconstruction quality and robustness to viewpoint variation with competitive computational efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [TabSOM: A tabular-to-image encoding method based on self-organizing maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13513)
*David Chushig-Muzo,María Ángeles Rodríguez de Cara,Eva Milara,Francisco J. Lara-Abelenda,Luis Zhinin-Vera,Diego H. Peluffo-Ordóñez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TabSOM是一种基于自组织映射(SOM)的表格数据到图像编码方法，通过将每个特征映射到固定画布位置并编码特征间的成对关系，在12种现有表格到图像方法的基准测试中表现最优或次优，同时提供两种可解释性分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格到图像方法虽然能将表格数据转换为图像以利用CNN和视觉Transformer的优势，但它们仅编码每个特征的边际值，丢弃了特征之间的关系信息，导致性能受限且缺乏可解释性。

Method: TabSOM基于自组织映射(SOM)构建，包含：(i)通过无碰撞匈牙利分配将每个输入特征放置在由组件平面导出的固定画布位置；(ii)从SOM组件平面导出的特征间成对关系图。生成的图像堆叠两个多尺度节点通道：一个编码特征值（固定尺度），另一个编码特征间交互（空间连接）。此外引入两种可解释性方法：原型启发的部分依赖图和类别分离重要性分数。

Result: 在12种现有表格到图像方法及公开二分类数据集的基准测试中，TabSOM在每个数据集上排名第一或第二，且方差最低。可解释性验证中，类别分离分数与Random Forest、XGBoost和SHAP在排名靠前特征上表现合理一致，同时捕获了输入数据中的互补结构信息。

Conclusion: TabSOM提供了将深度学习架构有效应用于表格数据且具备可解释性的方案，弥合了该领域中性能与可解释性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Tabular-to-image methods have emerged as novel approaches to leverage the high predictive performance of convolutional neural networks and vision transformers. They convert tabular data into image representations, mapping each feature at a fixed pixel location derived from a dimensionality-reduction method (e.g., t-SNE, UMAP, PCA). However, they encode only the marginal value of each feature and discard information about feature relationships. We propose TabSOM, a tabular-to-image encoding built on the Self-Organizing Map (SOM), which provides: (i) a spatial layout in which every input feature occupies a fixed canvas position derived from its component plane via collision-free Hungarian assignment; and (ii) a graph that captures pairwise feature relationships derived from the SOM component planes. The resulting image stacks two multi-scale node channels: one encodes feature values at fixed scales, while the other encodes pairwise feature interactions as spatial connections between related features. Two SOM-derived interpretability approaches are introduced: a prototype-inspired partial dependence plot and a class--separation importance score. Benchmarked against twelve existing tabular-to-image methods across public binary-classification datasets, TabSOM ranks first or second on every dataset and achieves the lowest variance of any method evaluated. Interpretability obtained with TabSOM was validated against Random Forest, XGBoost, and SHAP, the class-separation score shows reasonable agreement with established baselines on the top-ranked features while capturing complementary structural information from input data. These results demonstrate that TabSOM provides an effective and interpretable approach for applying deep learning architectures to tabular data, bridging the performance--interpretability gap in this domain.

</details>


### [87] [SCULPT: Subtractive Composition for 3D Part Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13541)
*Sikuang Li,Chen Yang,Jiemin Fang,Jiazhong Cen,Yuhe Wei,Jichen Pang,Wei Shen,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCULPT框架，通过减法合成（subtractive composition）实现部件感知的3D生成，在结构化3D潜空间中迭代使用联合分割预测器同步生成被提取的部分和剩余对象，避免传统分割法和加法合成法的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部件感知3D生成方法存在根本性缺陷：基于分割的方法先固定生成几何再划分部件，导致部件边界无法影响生成过程；基于加法合成的方法从预定义布局/框/token合成部件，虽暴露部件基数但共享边界容易出现缝隙、互穿和材质不连续等问题。

Method: SCULPT通过减法合成生成部件：在结构化3D潜空间中表示完整对象，迭代应用联合分割预测器，生成一个被提取的部件和剩余对象；预测器执行以图像和当前3D状态为条件的耦合去噪过程，使提取部件和更新后的剩余对象同步生成；联合分割预测器在两个输出的原生稀疏3D支撑并集上处理，允许相邻支撑重叠而非强制不相交体素划分；通过剩余支撑为空或达到固定安全上限来结束滚动，使部件数量在界内自适应于每个对象。

Result: 在PartObjaverse上取得了最先进的几何性能，同时保持部件组装后完整对象重建的强表现；在四个数据集图像、一个文本到图像生成输入和一个真实照片上的结果进一步展示了超越基准的细粒度带纹理部件分解。

Conclusion: SCULPT通过减法合成的范式解决了部件感知3D生成中分割方法与加法方法各自的固有问题，实现了部件边界与生成过程耦合、部件数量自适应以及鲁棒的共享边界处理，在基准测试和更广泛场景中均展现了优越性。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D generation aims to create digital assets that are coherent as complete objects while exposing structural parts for editing, material assignment, animation, and reuse. Existing methods impose this structure outside the native generation loop: segmentation-based methods partition an already generated shape, while additive methods synthesize parts from predefined layouts, boxes, or tokens and then reconcile them into a whole. The former preserves the generated geometry but fixes the object before part boundaries are determined; the latter exposes part cardinality but often leaves shared boundaries vulnerable to gaps, interpenetrations, and material discontinuities. In this paper, we propose SCULPT, a framework that addresses these challenges through subtractive composition. Given a complete object represented in a structured 3D latent space, SCULPT iteratively applies a joint split predictor to generate one extracted part together with the remaining object. The predictor performs a coupled denoising process conditioned on both the image and the current 3D state, so the extracted part and updated remainder are generated together rather than reconciled after generation. The joint split predictor processes both outputs on the union of their native sparse 3D supports, allowing neighboring supports to overlap rather than imposing a disjoint voxel partition. The rollout ends when the remainder support becomes empty or reaches a fixed safety cap, allowing the number of generated parts to adapt to each object within that bound. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art geometry on PartObjaverse while preserving strong complete-object reconstruction after part assembly. Results on four dataset images, one text-to-image-generated input, and one real-world photograph further show fine-grained textured part decomposition beyond the benchmark.

</details>


### [88] [Alaya-EVOKE: From Linear-Scaling Supervision to Endless World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13546)
*Yuanyang Yin,Gongxuan Wang,Yifan Zhan,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Evoke提出了一种外部化持久世界状态的交互式世界模型，通过相机索引的世界状态库保持去噪上下文有界，并重新设计了用于长时程监督的教师模型（稀疏注意力结合分块分组、检索远帧和线性注意力全局状态），通过30秒分布匹配目标将能力蒸馏到三步学生模型中，支持开放式的持续演进生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型需要同时满足持久记忆、响应式交互和长时程生成，但现有方法面临两个核心矛盾：(1) 在去噪器上下文或KV缓存中维护历史会带来线性增长的代价，迫使会话长度与记忆保真度之间做出权衡；(2) 低延迟交互依赖少步生成，但其能力受限于教师模型的短视监督，短窗口内局部合理的生成容易产生长期漂移。

Method: (1) 将场景几何外部化为相机索引的世界状态库，仅检索与当前视角相关的信息，使去噪器上下文在会话增长时任然保持有界；(2) 将教师模型重构为长时程监督设计：稀疏注意力组合分块分组（chunk-wise grouping）、检索选定远帧和线性注意力全局状态，使记忆和计算复杂度线性增长，支持长时程监督；(3) 应用30秒分布匹配目标，在自强制展开（self-forced rollouts）下将长时程能力蒸馏到不使用分类器引导的三步学生模型，同时通过分块条件化支持提示切换和事件控制。

Result: 在上下文有界和循环外部记忆的条件下，Evoke支持开放式持续演进生成；在单张H200上以384×640分辨率，每个1.5秒的块生成仅需2.11秒。作为三步世界模型，Evoke在WBench上达到最先进性能，同时在VBench-Long和VBench-2.0上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过外部化世界状态和重新设计教师监督范式，Evoke有效解决了交互式世界模型中记忆与延迟之间的根本矛盾，在实现低延迟响应式交互的同时保持了长时程生成的一致性，为开放世界的长时程交互式生成提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: Interactive world models must support persistent memory, responsive interaction, and long-horizon generation, yet these requirements place conflicting demands on the model. Maintaining history in the denoiser context or key-value cache incurs growing cost, forcing a trade-off between session length and retained memory, while low-latency interaction relies on few-step generation whose capabilities are bounded by its teacher. Evoke addresses both limitations by externalizing persistent world state and redesigning the teacher for long-horizon interactive generation. Scene geometry is maintained in an external, camera-indexed world state bank, from which only view-relevant information is retrieved, keeping the denoiser context bounded as the session grows. Rather than treating the teacher as a fixed generator, we design it for long-horizon supervision: its sparse attention combines chunk-wise grouping, retrieval of selected distant frames, and a linear-attention global state, yielding linear growth in memory and compute while enabling supervision over long horizons. Such supervision exposes content drift that stays locally plausible within short windows, while per-chunk conditioning enables prompt changes and event control throughout the sequence. A 30-second distribution-matching objective, applied under self-forced rollouts, transfers both capabilities to a three-step student that uses no classifier-free guidance, improving resistance to long-term drift while preserving responsive conditioning. With bounded context and recurrent external memory, Evoke supports open-ended, continuously evolving generation; on a single H200 at $384\times 640$, each $1.5\,\mathrm{s}$ chunk is generated in $2.11\,\mathrm{s}$. As a three-step world model, Evoke achieves state-of-the-art performance on WBench while remaining competitive on VBench-Long and VBench-2.0.

</details>


### [89] [PlayWorld: Benchmarking World Models with Agent Players over Long-Horizon Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13552)
*Kaixin Ding,Xi Chen,Minghong Cai,Zhiyuan Xu,Yiyang Wang,Yuxiang Lu,Junyi Li,Shuyang Chen,Yuan Gao,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PlayWorld基准测试，利用多模态Agent Player与视频世界模型交互，以评估其在长时间跨度目标下的表现，涵盖171个场景和四个核心维度（几何一致性、交互逼真度、视野外演变、洞察演变）以及基础能力指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的固定动作条件评估方式不适合跨模型比较，因为不同世界模型为达成相同目标可能需要完全不同的动作序列；而实际上人类玩家通过长期目标驱动交互来评估模型，因此需要一种更公平、更贴近真实使用的评估范式。

Method: 采用多模态Agent Player与世界模型进行长期目标驱动的交互；构建PlayWorld基准，包含171个带明确目标的场景；从几何一致性、交互逼真度、视野外演变和洞察演变四个维度进行评估，并结合视频质量和可控性等基本能力指标。

Result: 在九个最先进的世界模型上的实验表明，当前模型在长期交互目标上仍不可靠，尤其在维持空间一致性和持续性状态演变方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: 现有的视频世界模型虽在短期视频质量和动作可控性上表现良好，但在长期交互任务中空间一致性和状态连贯性仍是关键瓶颈，亟需进一步改进。

Abstract: Video world models simulate future states conditioned on current observations and user actions. Recent systems have demonstrated impressive video consistency and action controllability over long sequences. However, fairly comparing these interactive models remains challenging. In practice, a human player typically evaluates a world model by pursuing long-horizon objectives through interaction. For example, a user may turn around 360 degrees to see whether the environment remains consistent, or walk into the water and inspect whether realistic water ripples are generated. The action sequence required to achieve the same objective may vary substantially between models, making fixed action-conditioned evaluation unsuitable for cross-model comparison. To address this, we employ multi-modal Agent Players to interact with world models toward specified long-horizon objectives. Building on this paradigm, we introduce PlayWorld, a benchmark providing 171 scenarios, each with a specified objective. To evaluate performance thoroughly, we assess models along four core dimensions: geometry consistency, interaction fidelity, out-of-sight evolution, and insight evolution. In addition, we incorporate basic ability metrics for video quality and controllability. Experiments across nine state-of-the-art world models reveal that current models remain unreliable on long-horizon interactive objectives, particularly in maintaining spatial consistency and persistent state evolution. Code and data are available at https://github.com/kxding/PlayWorld.

</details>


### [90] [V-RAE: Rethinking Video Latent Spaces for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13556)
*Minghui Guo,Shengqiong Wu,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: V-RAE是一个视频表示自编码器，在冻结的视觉基础模型表示之上构建紧凑的生成式潜空间，通过轻量级时序池化模块去除时序冗余并保留语义结构，在视频重建、语义探测和类条件生成上均取得优于现有大规模预训练视频VAE的效果，并引入tFVD作为更可靠的时序一致性诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频自编码器的潜空间主要针对像素级重建进行优化，缺乏高层语义组织。然而，重建最优的潜空间并不一定适合生成建模，这一问题在视频领域尤为突出。作者旨在构建一个既紧凑又具备语义结构、同时适用于生成任务的视频潜空间。

Method: 提出V-RAE：在冻结的视觉基础模型表示上构建生成式潜空间。具体包括：(1) 轻量级时序池化模块，用于去除时序冗余同时保留语义结构；(2) 视频解码器，从压缩特征重建连续运动。在生成评估中采用匹配的生成设置，并引入tFVD（时序一致性诊断指标）。

Result: V-RAE在K600上达到2.13 rFVD，优于所有评估的大规模预训练视频VAE；潜空间保留显著更多语义信息；在匹配生成设置下，最优变体在UCF101和K600上分别达到117.86和19.16的gFVD，收敛速度最高提升6倍；在Cityscapes未来视频预测任务上优于Wan 2.2 VAE潜空间。

Conclusion: 实验表明，仅靠重建质量不足以刻画生成的实用性，冻结的语义表示可以同时支持视频重建、生成和预测建模。V-RAE证明了语义感知的潜空间对生成建模的价值。

Abstract: Latent video generation relies on autoencoders to define a compact space in which generative models operate. Although video autoencoder architectures have evolved substantially, their latent spaces are still optimized primarily for pixel-level reconstruction and provide limited high-level semantic organization. A reconstruction-optimal latent space, however, need not be well suited to generative modeling. We propose V-RAE, a video representation autoencoder that builds compact generative latents on top of frozen vision foundation model representations. A lightweight temporal pooling module removes temporal redundancy while preserving semantic structure, and a video decoder reconstructs continuous motion from the compressed features. We evaluate V-RAE with four representative frozen encoders on video reconstruction, semantic probing, and class-conditional generation. V-RAE achieves 2.13 rFVD on K600, outperforming all evaluated large-scale pretrained video VAEs. Its latents retain substantially more semantic information than conventional video tokenizer latents. Under matched generation settings, our best variant achieves gFVD scores of 117.86 and 19.16 on UCF101 and K600, respectively, while converging up to 6x faster}. We further show that reconstruction quality alone is insufficient to characterize generative utility and introduce tFVD, a temporal-coherence diagnostic that correlates more reliably with downstream generation quality. Beyond video generation, V-RAE also improves future video prediction on Cityscapes over the Wan 2.2 VAE latent space under matched prediction settings. Taken together, the experiments show that frozen semantic representations can support video reconstruction, generation, and predictive modeling. The project page: https://v-rae.github.io/.

</details>


### [91] [AutoDesign: Meta-Harness Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13560)
*Yaxin Luo,Haobin Jiang,Jialv Zou,Xu Huang,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Jing Li,Xiaofu Chen,Xiaohan Zhao,Jiacheng Liu,Jiacheng Cui,Zhiqiang Shen,Xiaotong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoDesign框架，将多模态源转化为结构化媒体输出的过程概念化为长周期代理过程，通过元harness优化器引导代码代理基于rollout反馈递归改进harness系统。在论文转海报任务上，AutoDesign超越商业系统Claude Design 7.45分，并验证了学习到的DesignHarness在多种配置下的通用提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的harness系统是静态的，无法与人类设计先验对齐，也无法通过经验积累实现递归自改进。理想的harness系统应能积累可重用经验并通过实证探索驱动递归式自我提升。

Method: 提出AutoDesign框架，包含一个元harness优化器，引导代码代理基于rollout反馈对harness进行递归改进。同时构建PosterBench基准（含100篇论文的Main Track和10篇论文的mini子集）用于评测论文转海报生成任务。

Result: 在PosterBench Main Track上AutoDesign取得78.32分，超越Claude Design 7.45分；在7种受控配置中集成DesignHarness使平均分数从54.99提升至67.39（+12.4%）；在完全自主的长周期循环中以不到3美元成本在40分钟内执行253次工具调用和11次编辑，达到平均会议海报质量；系统盲测中获最高人类偏好。

Conclusion: AutoDesign验证了通过元优化与反馈驱动的递归改进范式，能够构建与人类设计先验对齐且可自改进的harness系统，在多模态媒体生成任务中显著超越现有商业系统。

Abstract: Transforming multimodal sources into condensed and structured media outputs can be fundamentally conceptualized as a long-horizon agentic process centered on a model-harness system. While an ideal harness system should align with human design priors and accumulate reusable experience through empirical exploration to drive recursive self-improvement, existing paradigms remain static and fall short of this capability. In this paper, we present AutoDesign, a framework that aligns with human design priors, where a meta-harness optimizer guides a code agent to recursively improve harness based on rollout feedback. To instantiate and evaluate this framework, we focus on the academic paper-to-poster generation task and introduce PosterBench, comprising a 100-paper Main Track spanning five disciplines and PosterBench-mini, a shared 10-paper subset for controlled evaluation. On the PosterBench Main Track, AutoDesign achieves the highest score of 78.32, surpassing the closed-source commercial system Claude Design by 7.45 points. Across seven controlled code-agent-model configurations, integrating the learned DesignHarness consistently improves performance, increasing the average PosterBench Score from 54.99 to 67.39 (+12.4%). In a fully autonomous long-horizon loop, it executes 253 tool calls and 11 editing turns within 40 minutes for under $3, reaching average conference-poster quality in human evaluation. A system-blind human study further demonstrates that AutoDesign achieves the highest human preference among evaluated systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张当前AI推理研究缺乏统一定义，导致评估的构念效度无法验证。作者提出了基于文献综合的操作性定义，将有效且可靠的推理定位为可学习的基于规则的过程，并提供了一份AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式AI在推理领域进展迅速，但社区未能在"推理"的操作性定义上达成共识，且往往忽略了逻辑学和可验证自动推理领域对这一主题的历史性处理。这种定义模糊性使得推理评估的构念效度（construct validity）无法验证，阻碍了向可信、自主推理方向的可量化进展。

Method: (1) 通过综合文献，提出操作性定义，将有效且可靠（valid and sound）的推理定位为一种可学习的基于规则的过程（learnable rule-based process）；(2) 提供用于AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践检查清单（checklist）。

Result: 文章提出了一套操作性定义和最佳实践沟通清单，旨在解决推理定义模糊问题，使推理评估的构念效度变得可验证。

Conclusion: 定义模糊是可解决的，通过提供清晰的操作性定义和沟通规范，可以为可信自主推理的研究建立可量化、可验证的基础。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [93] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了IntegrityBench基准，用于评估语言模型在机构压力下维护科研诚信的能力。通过对18个前沿模型变体、36个配对任务的评估发现，在最大压力下模型约1/3的诚信关键决策失败，且模型规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解；显性压力诱发不当行为遵从，隐性情境重构更易导致合法研究任务的过度拒绝；三个诚信维度（不当行为分类、伦理行动推理、面向证据决策）在结构上彼此分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型正越来越多地作为共同科学家（co-scientist）被部署，但其在机构压力下维护科研诚信的能力尚未得到量化评估。现有研究缺乏针对科研诚信的标准化基准，也无法区分不同类型的压力（显性与隐性）对模型行为的影响，更未探究诚信判定各维度之间的内在关联。

Method: 提出IntegrityBench基准，包含36个配对任务，涵盖3个科研领域和4个研究阶段，采用5级显性-隐性压力协议。基准评估三个维度：不当行为分类、伦理行动推理和面向证据（artifact-grounded）的决策制定。对18个前沿模型变体进行系统评测，并比较不同压力水平和不同诚信维度下的模型表现。

Result: 在峰值压力下，模型在约1/3的诚信关键决策上失败；模型规模和推理能力无法可靠地缓解这一现象。显性压力诱导模型遵从不当行为，而隐性情境重构更多导致模型对合法研究任务的过度拒绝。值得注意的是，在学术请求分类上表现不佳的模型，在面向证据的决策上反而表现相当或更好（85.7 vs 79.4），表明三个维度在结构上相互分离，正确的伦理行动并不依赖于准确的分类。

Conclusion: 前沿模型可能在表面上显得有帮助，但潜藏着诚信失败，这产生两类不同的部署风险：一是助长科研不端行为，二是侵蚀对AI辅助研究的信任。研究强调需要将诚信评估作为语言模型部署前的独立维度加以考量。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [94] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，论证现代AI对齐方法（原本用于防止有害输出）具有双重用途，可能被恶意行为者滥用为审查和操控工具。作者通过将当前对齐技术与可能及实际的滥用案例进行映射，指出追求"完美对齐"的模型无意中为恶意行为者提供了不断改进的信息主导工具，并呼吁学界关注这一双重用途风险并提出缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI作为信息提供者被快速广泛采用，加上经济权力不对称和日益集权化的政治环境，AI对齐机制的双重用途风险正在加剧。作者认为必须立即讨论这一问题，因为它不仅影响技术安全，更关乎信息自由和社会民主。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）的论证方法，通过系统性映射当前AI对齐技术到可能的滥用场景及实际案例，分析双重用途潜力，并从用户采用速度、经济权力不对称、政治动向三个维度评估风险加剧因素。

Result: 论证表明，"完美对齐"模型的追求在客观上为恶意行为者提供了日益强大的信息主导工具，对齐技术在防止有害输出的同时，其机制本身也可能被逆向利用进行审查和操控。

Conclusion: 作者敦促AI社区正视对齐机制被故意滥用的可能性，并提出了缓解策略，以防范这一双重用途潜力带来的风险。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [95] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个基于ETHICS的500条道德判断基准，比较人类标注者与LLM在最终标签和支撑理由两个层面的一致性，发现即使最终标签高度一致，模型与人类在道德理由（如伤害、尊重、正义等）的权重分配上存在系统性分歧，表明标签一致性不能等同于对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估常用“与人类判断的一致性”作为LLM对齐的代理指标，但一致性的最终标签并不能说明人类和模型依赖相同的道德理由或原则。两个主体可能基于不同的原则、语境假设或情境解释得出相同判断。作者旨在揭示这种“标签一致但理由分歧”的现象。

Method: 构建一个基于ETHICS的500项基准，涵盖五个道德判断领域，收集新的人类标注者和多种LLM（包括前沿与开源模型）的最终标签及支撑理由标注。在标签层面计算与人类多数意见的一致性，并在理由层面进行系统性比较分析，考察模型在不同道德类别（伤害、尊重、承诺、正义、应得、借口相关性）上的注意力分配。

Result: 前沿和开源模型与人类多数标签的一致性通常很高，但理由层面分析显示人类标注者与模型在道德理由上存在系统性分歧。模型在多个道德类别上的注意力分配与人类不同，即便最终标签与人类多数一致时也是如此。

Conclusion: 一致性不能等同于对齐。基于标签的评估可能产生误导性的乐观结论，必须辅以对模型判断中表达的理由、原则和道德优先级的分析。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [96] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream，一种面向异构移动边缘-云基础设施的流处理调度系统。其核心贡献是LLM-MR-CNP：对经典合同网协议（CNP）的扩展，融合了语义CFP公式、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证。边缘集群代理基于本地观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文，精炼自然语言卸载提案，而硬性资源与QoS约束保持确定性。基于阿里ASI跟踪数据的实验表明，该系统将延迟违规降至3%、消除资源过度承诺、在20个代理下达到0.91的冲突解决率，并比多轮规则基线提升最多22%的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统日益运行在异构移动边缘-云基础设施上，面临工作负载波动、资源争用和严格的QoS要求，使分散式调度（decentralized scheduling）变得复杂。现有方法难以同时处理定性上下文（如自然语言描述的运行时状态）与确定性的资源/QoS硬约束。

Method: 提出MAS-DecStream框架，核心为LLM-MR-CNP，是经典Contract Net Protocol的扩展，包含五大组件：语义CFP（调用征求意见）公式、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证。边缘集群代理利用大语言模型从本地观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文精炼自然语言卸载提案，同时保持资源与QoS约束的确定性约束。

Result: 实验基于阿里ASI Trace，在三个层面评估：单轮vs多轮CNP、规则vs LLM辅助精炼、固定模型单轮vs多轮协商。结果显示：延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个代理下冲突解决率达0.91，效用相比多轮规则基线提升最高22%。另有一个25案例的评估揭示了模型和提示依赖的准确性-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 结果表明，多轮CNP精炼是协议层面的主要收益来源，而LLM辅助在处理定性和不确定性运行时上下文方面增加额外价值。这为在边缘-云流处理环境中结合符号协议与LLM能力提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [97] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知对齐"概念，主张在高风险决策场景中，AI系统不仅需要行为正确，还应以与用户相似的方式推理并忠实地传达其推理过程，以提升可理解性和可信度，并推动AI的实际采用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地充当决策辅助、决策代理或完全自主决策者，现有的对齐方法主要关注行为层面的对齐，忽略了认知层面的对齐。在高风险决策场景中，用户需要理解AI的推理过程才能合理信任其判断。认知错位可能成为AI在实际应用中广泛采用的主要障碍。

Method: 本文是一篇立场论文，主要通过三方面展开：(1) 综述已有证据，说明认知对齐能提升AI系统的可理解性和可信赖性；(2) 提供新的调查数据，显示许多用户将认知对齐视为"必要"——特别是当他们重视AI判断或行动的理由时；(3) 系统梳理现有对齐方法与实现认知对齐所需方法之间的差距，并提出针对性研究议程。

Result: 调查数据表明，当AI推理过程对用户重要时，认知对齐被许多用户视为"必不可少"。同时，论文识别出现有对齐方法在认知层面存在明显不足，无法直接满足认知对齐的需求。

Conclusion: 认知错位很可能是AI在许多预期应用中的采用障碍。实现认知对齐对创造用户既愿意又可合理依赖的AI系统至关重要，应作为未来研究的重要方向。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [98] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）在高风险战略决策中的行为是否受提示语言影响。通过让模型为核武器国家是否攻击无防御对手提供建议，发现日语提示显著降低了Claude和Gemini模型家族的发射率，且机制在于模型被要求用日语推理时自发产生道德词汇，而非输入语言本身。英语单一语言评估可能遗漏其他语言中编码的风险与保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于战略和咨询场景，但安全对齐评估通常仅用英语进行。这一评估缺口可能导致对模型安全行为的误判——模型在英语中表现安全，但在其他语言中可能表现不同，从而在高风险场景中带来未被发现的危险。

Method: 测试了来自6个提供商的9个模型，使用单轮博弈论情境（vignette），让模型为核武国家是否攻击无防御对手提供建议。提示在跨语言间刻意保持非道德化和战略上相同的设计。通过对比不同语言提示下的发射率，并进行跨语言实验（在英语提示中要求用日语推理）来隔离语言机制的作用。

Result: 日语提示显著降低Claude模型家族的发射率：Claude Sonnet 4.6在不需要打击的场景中从40%降至0%，在争议场景中从93%降至17%，而在战略理性场景中影响甚微。Gemini Pro 3.1同样从53%降至13%。跨语言实验发现：指令用日语推理时发射率从93%降至37%，证明是推理语言而非输入语言驱动该效应。用日语推理时模型自发产生提示中完全不存在的道德词汇（如"道德代价"、"百万生命"）。其他5个模型在几乎所有条件下都会发射，且无语言效应。该效应需要模型在英语中已有犹豫倾向。

Conclusion: LLM的安全行为具有语言依赖性，仅用英语评估安全对齐可能遗漏其他语言中编码的风险与保障。安全评估框架应扩展至多语言场景，同时需关注模型的推理语言（而非仅输入语言）对安全决策的影响。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [99] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Flow Transformer，一种双流语言模型架构，其主流动处理prompt并维护KV缓存，辅流动仅在续写阶段激活以增加解码计算量，实现了推理阶段（prefill vs decode）计算资源的独立分配，在多MoE和密集架构实验中展示了更低的验证损失和灵活的专家分配能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM推理请求增多，累积推理成本相对一次性训练成本愈发重要。传统宽度或深度扩展会同时增加prefill和decode两个阶段的成本，因为每层在两个阶段都被计算。作者希望将额外的学习计算专门分配给延续预测（decode），而不影响prompt-wide的主要计算和KV缓存。

Method: Dual-Flow Transformer包含两个流：主流动是完整的因果语言模型，负责处理prompt并写入KV缓存；辅助流动在prompt处理阶段被省略，仅从最终prompt位置开始激活，增加续写预测的计算量但不写入持久状态、不影响主流动。两个流共享主要的attention、MLP和输出矩阵，但使用独立的token嵌入和轻量耦合。在MoE模型中，主辅流的专家fan-out可以独立控制，分别调节prefill成本、续写成本和预测质量。

Result: 在匹配token数量的对比实验中，Dual-Flow在不同架构和数据配置下均取得更低的验证损失。在MoE模型中，实验揭示了prefill-decode质量权衡，并展示了相位特定专家分配（增加decode计算而不增加prefill专家计算，以及在两个流之间重新分配固定decode专家预算）的潜力。

Conclusion: 通过在推理的两个阶段（prefill和decode）之间解耦计算资源的分配，Dual-Flow Transformer提供了一种更高效的推理计算分配方式，在保持单KV缓存和权重共享的同时，实现了更优的预测质量和灵活的相位特定计算预算控制。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases. We ask whether additional learned computation can instead be allocated to continuation prediction while preserving the prompt-wide primary computation and a single persistent key-value (KV) cache. We introduce the Dual-Flow Transformer. Its primary flow is a complete causal language model that processes the prompt and writes the KV cache. The auxiliary flow is omitted during prompt processing and activated only from the final prompt position onward, adding continuation-prediction computation without writing persistent state or influencing the primary flow. The two flows share major attention, MLP, and output matrices, while using separate token embeddings and lightweight coupling. Sharing weights and the primary cache also creates opportunities to reuse loaded weights and cached keys and values during grouped execution. Across matched-token comparisons, Dual-Flow achieves lower validation loss across architectures and data configurations. In MoE models, the separation makes primary and auxiliary expert fan-outs independent controls over prompt cost, continuation cost, and predictive quality. We study two regimes: increasing decode computation at fixed prefill expert computation, and reallocating a fixed decode expert budget between the two flows. These experiments expose a prefill-decode-quality trade-off and demonstrate the potential of phase-specific expert allocation.

</details>


### [100] [Learning to Adapt Cross-Domain Preferences via Meta-LoRA for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12389)
*Xuefei Wang,Jun Han,Zixuan Wang,Qingkai Zeng,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA方法，用于跨域零样本或少样本个性化对话生成，通过元学习的LoRA初始化作为起点与先验中心，并根据支持集规模和预测不确定性调节更新强度，同时将个性化先验功能分解为用户和领域两组件，在多个基准上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域零样本/少样本个性化旨在仅凭少量目标域交互生成用户偏好的回复，但现有适应方法在稀疏证据下难以校准更新幅度导致过拟合，而历史迁移方法易将用户偏好与源域伪影纠缠，造成不可靠的个性化先验和负迁移。

Method: 提出PAC-Bayes正则化Meta-LoRA方法：(1) 使用元学习的LoRA初始化作为适应起点和先验中心；(2) 根据支持集大小和预测不确定性自适应调整更新强度，避免稀疏或模糊证据下的过拟合；(3) 将个性化先验功能分解为用户组件（人类可读的稳定偏好提示）和领域组件（拓扑保持的软令牌用于领域特定的隐空间条件化）。

Result: 在多个基准和个性化任务上一致优于强基线。在HiCUPID上，相对最优竞争基线，跨域胜率劣化降低47.9%，未见用户冷启动场景下胜率提升110.2%。

Conclusion: 通过PAC-Bayes约束校准适应力度并结合功能解耦的个性化先验分解，可以有效缓解跨域个性化中的过拟合和负迁移问题，实现稀疏证据下可控且可靠的个性化适应。

Abstract: Cross-domain zero- or few-shot personalization aims to generate user-preferred responses in unseen conversational domains from only a handful of target-domain interactions. Existing adaptation methods struggle to calibrate update magnitude under sparse evidence and thus overfit, whereas history-transfer methods often entangle user preferences with source-domain artifacts, yielding unreliable personalization priors and negative transfer. To calibrate adaptation to evidence quality, we propose PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA, which uses a meta-learned LoRA initialization as both the adaptation start and prior center, while adjusting update strength according to support-set size and predictive uncertainty. This limits overfitting under sparse or ambiguous evidence while permitting stronger personalization as evidence grows. Controlled adaptation alone does not determine which preferences should transfer across domains or how they should be expressed. We therefore functionally decompose personalization priors into user and domain components, using a human-readable prompt for stable preferences and topology-preserving soft tokens for domain-specific hidden-space conditioning. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and personalization tasks show consistent gains over strong baselines. On HiCUPID, our method reduces cross-domain win-rate degradation by 47.9% relative to the best competing baseline and improves win rate by 110.2% under unseen-user cold start.

</details>


### [101] [Research Assistant: AstraZeneca's Agentic System for R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12395)
*Piotr Grabowski,Mohamed Alameen,Jorge Bretones,Sabina Cardell,Miguel Carmona,Gavin Edwards,Ben Grainger,Sameh Hassan,Erik Jansson,Artur Kuziakhmetov,Albert Maristany,Hebatallah Mohamed,Andriy Nikolov,Sebastian Nilsson,Mark O'Donoghue,James Pacileo,Ashiq Sultan,Alex Voegele,Michael Ughetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 AstraZeneca 内部开发的基于 LLM 的研究助手系统，该系统通过聊天界面整合科学文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全性资源、表达数据和内部实验系统等多源异构数据，支持快速问答和复杂多步研究任务，并提供有据可查的回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助科学家和临床研究人员在广泛的异构生物医学数据源中高效探索问题，将分散的文献、数据、系统整合到一个统一的问答平台中，以支持日常研发工作流程。

Method: 系统采用聊天式界面，融合多类数据源（文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全资源、表达数据、内部实验系统）；设计双模式架构：快速模式用于直接问答，多步模式用于复杂研究任务；回答基于检索证据并链接回原始来源。

Result: 系统已在大规模部署中支持 AstraZeneca 日常研发工作流程，本文概述了系统架构、产品设计决策及规模化部署中获得的经验教训。

Conclusion: 该内部 LLM 系统成功整合多源异构生物医学数据并提供可溯源的问答能力，其实践经验对未来在制药研发领域构建和部署类似 LLM 系统具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: We describe Research Assistant, an internal LLM-based system developed at AstraZeneca to help scientists and clinicians explore biomedical questions across a broad range of data sources. The system provides a chat-style interface that brings together evidence from scientific literature, knowledge graphs, chemistry, clinical trials, safety resources, expression data, and internal experimental systems. It supports both a fast mode for direct question answering and a multi-step mode for more complex research tasks. Responses are grounded in retrieved evidence and linked back to the original sources, allowing users to review and further explore the underlying data. In this technical note, we outline the system architecture, the main design choices behind the product, and lessons learned from deploying it at scale to support day-to-day R&D workflows across AstraZeneca.

</details>


### [102] [Large Language Models Can Follow Instructions, But Not Many at Once: Phase Transitions in Compositional Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12426)
*Mariya I. Vasileva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了约束饱和评估（CSE）基准，系统性地研究了大型语言模型在同时满足多个约束时的性能退化现象。通过对15个模型、36种约束类型、369,753次检查（k=1-12）的实验，发现单约束通过率随约束数量渐进而可预测地衰减，但全部约束同时满足的成功率呈指数级崩塌。约束类型退化不均匀（结构性约束比词汇性约束退化更快），失败几乎独立导致累积乘法效应，可靠指令跟随在5-6个约束以上即发生崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在实际部署中常需同时满足多种显式约束（推理结构、安全边界、输出模式），但现有研究主要关注单一约束的处理能力。组合约束场景下性能如何退化、退化由什么因素决定、能否缓解这些问题缺乏系统性研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出约束饱和评估（CSE）基准，采用程序化生成方式系统变化同时约束数量（k=1-12）。每个约束由确定性、基于规则的验证器评分，完全不依赖LLM作为评判者。实验覆盖15个模型、36种约束类型，共369,753次检查。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1) 单约束通过率随k增加逐渐且可预测衰减，但全部约束同时通过的概率呈崩溃式下降——在k=8时单约束通过率约41%的模型，全部通过仅5.7%；(2) 约束退化不均衡，结构性约束每个新增约束的基线能力损失是词汇性约束的2倍，区分因素在于需要持续追踪的"理解维持"差距；(3) 失败几乎互相独立，使累积呈乘法效应，残余耦合源于共享输出特征而非成对干扰。最强模型在7个约束时探针级成功率跌破50%，12/15的模型在3个或更少约束时即失败。

Conclusion: 可靠的指令跟随在超过5-6个同时约束时会发生系统性崩溃，这一发现对LLM在复杂多约束场景中的实际部署提出了重要警示，并呼吁需要新的缓解策略来应对组合约束下的性能崩塌。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings that require simultaneous adherence to multiple explicit constraints - reasoning structure, safety boundaries, output schemas. Individual constraints are handled proficiently, but the compositional regime, where many must hold jointly, remains poorly characterized: how rapidly does performance degrade, what governs the degradation, and can the collapse be mitigated? We introduce Constraint Saturation Evaluation (CSE), a procedurally generated benchmark that systematically varies the number of simultaneous constraints (k), with every constraint scored by a deterministic, rule-based verifier and zero LLM-judge involvement: 15 models, 36 constraint types, 369,753 checks at k=1-12. Three findings emerge. First, per-constraint pass rate decays gradually and predictably, while the chance of satisfying all k constraints collapses - a model passing individual constraints at ~41% at k=8 succeeds on all eight just 5.7% of the time. Second, constraints do not degrade equally: structural constraints lose 2x more baseline capability per added constraint than lexical ones, ordered by a comprehension-maintenance gap that separates constraints requiring sustained tracking from binary decisions immune to composition. Third, failures are nearly independent, which is what makes the accumulation multiplicative; the residual coupling that does exist tracks shared output features rather than pairwise interference - a wrong sentence count fails every constraint that reads it. Reliable instruction following breaks down beyond 5-6 simultaneous constraints: probe-level success falls below 50% at 7 constraints for the strongest model, and at 3 or fewer for 12 of 15.

</details>


### [103] [MindMemOS: A Portable and Self-Evolving Memory Operating Layer for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12428)
*Kaichao Liang,Yuqi Cui,Hao Kong,Xinyuan Huang,Guohaotian Hou,Qingcan Kang,Liang Chen,Yiyang Yin,Ke Ye,Jiaquan Guo,Da Chen,Lingan Zeng,Yixing Peng,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindMemOS是一个可移植的、自我进化的AI代理记忆操作系统，通过统一实体属性时间结构组织开放世界信息，采用验证驱动的进化搜索（MindMemEvolve）优化记忆模式，并利用"梦境"机制合并冗余记录、解决冲突，通过隐式纠正反馈实现人机协同记忆修正，以及MindSkillEvolve算法将执行轨迹转化为可复用的渐进式技能。在LOCOMO上达到94.03%精度，在PersonaMem上达到70.63%，MindSkillEvolve在SpreadsheetBench上将成功率较初始技能基线提升9.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理的记忆系统在开发后往往是固定的，无法通过持续使用来自适应地调整记忆模型、组织策略和程序性知识，这限制了代理在长期交互中的适应能力和个性化程度。

Method: 提出MindMemOS，一种可移植且自我进化的记忆操作系统层。核心方法包括：(1) 统一实体属性时间结构组织开放世界信息；(2) MindMemEvolve算法采用验证驱动的进化搜索优化目标场景的记忆模式；(3) "梦境"（dreaming）机制通过合并冗余记录和解决冲突来巩固积累的记忆；(4) 隐式纠正反馈作为人在环路信号识别和修订不准确或不对齐的记忆；(5) MindSkillEvolve算法将代理执行轨迹转化为可复用且逐步精炼的技能。

Result: MindMemOS在LOCOMO数据集上达到94.03%的准确率，在PersonaMem数据集上达到70.63%的准确率；MindSkillEvolve在SpreadsheetBench上将成功率较初始技能基线提升了9.2个百分点。

Conclusion: MindMemOS通过自我进化的记忆管理机制（包括模式优化、记忆巩固、错误修正和技能演化）显著提升了AI代理的记忆适应能力和任务执行性能，验证了记忆系统从静态存储向动态自我进化范式转变的可行性。

Abstract: Memory is a core component of AI agents, enabling them to accumulate experience, maintain personalization, and adapt over long-term interactions. However, existing memory systems often remain fixed after development, limiting their ability to adapt their memory models, organization strategies, and procedural knowledge through continued use. We present MindMemOS, a portable and self-evolving memory operating layer that organizes open-world information using a unified entity property timestructure. MindMemOS supports scenario-adaptive memory modeling, higher-order pattern discovery, autonomous memory refinement, and continuous skill evolution. Its MindMemEvolve algorithm employs validation-driven evolutionary search to optimize memory schemas for target scenarios, whiledreaming consolidates accumulated memories by merging redundant records and resolving conflicts. In addition, implicit corrective feedback serves as a human-in-the-loop signal for identifying and revising potentially inaccurate or misaligned memories. Its MindSkillEvolve algorithm further transforms agent execution trajectories into reusable and progressively refined skills. MindMemOS achieves 94.03% accuracy on LOCOMO and 70.63% on PersonaMem. MindSkillEvolve improves SpreadsheetBench success by 9.2 percentage points over the initial-skill baseline.

</details>


### [104] [Governed Persistent Memory: Source-Bound State Semantics and Fail-Closed Release for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12476)
*Guodong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GPM（受治理的持久记忆）模型，一种可审计的双时态状态转换系统，用于解决长时智能体记忆中检索阶段未决的冲突、过期、撤回和删除记录问题。通过五条可执行契约和fail-closed结构化释放机制，在冻结基准和密封服务评估中达到高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统长时智能体记忆采用"选择-存储-检索"范式，但检索阶段无法判断矛盾、已取代、被撤回、被删除或过时的记录是否可用于支持外发声明，缺乏对记忆生命周期状态的严格治理和可审计性。

Method: GPM采用可审计的双时态状态转换模型，包含源绑定准入、派生生命周期状态、当前公共屏障和fail-closed结构化释放。五条可执行契约覆盖：账本完整性、源绑定、冲突隔离、撤回/删除后不复活、以及在已验证头部上的新鲜视图上的精确声明闭合。通过有限模型探索和差分测试进行验证。

Result: 在3,600例GPM-ReleaseBench中，GPM匹配全部完整结果（最强简单策略仅匹配1,800/3,600且50%违规用例产生未匹配释放）。密封的V3端到端评估中，受治理通道2,400/2,400正确，而未治理的Qwen2.5-7B仅600/2,400，修复1,800个基线失败且无回归（单侧95%下界99.875%和99.834%）。V5重新密封同样每臂2,400/2,400。有限模型探索331,776语义态和1,990,656查询态无完整契约反例；100,000迹三引擎差分零不匹配。

Conclusion: GPM在受治理的记忆生命周期管理方面取得了强约束下的正确性保证，但结果是有界契约和实现层面的，不声称开放世界模型准确率或世界真实性。受治理答案是确定性的服务输出，7B结果仅作为未治理对比基线的参照。

Abstract: Long-term agent memory is usually treated as select--store--retrieve, but retrieval does not decide whether contradictory, superseded, retracted, deleted, or stale records may support an outgoing claim. We introduce Governed Persistent Memory (GPM), an auditable bitemporal state-transition model with source-bound admission, derived lifecycle state, current public barriers, and fail-closed structured release. Five executable clauses cover ledger integrity, source binding, conflict isolation, non-revival after retraction or deletion, and exact claim closure over a fresh view at one verified head.
  On a prespecified hash-frozen 3,600-case GPM-ReleaseBench, GPM matches all complete outcomes; the strongest of three intentionally simple complete policies matches 1,800/3,600 and makes unmatched releases on 50% of violation cases. A separate sealed end-to-end service evaluation exercises real ingestion and release across eight query families. In its publicly disclosed V3 arm, the governed lane is correct on 2,400/2,400 clusters versus 600/2,400 for ungoverned local Qwen2.5-7B; it repairs all 1,800 baseline failures with no regression (one-sided 95% lower bounds 99.875% and 99.834%). A later V5 reseal over Chinese- and English-command arms, with generation-date pinning and no post-freeze reducer amendment, again obtains 2,400/2,400 per arm. A production-code-independent finite model explores 331,776 semantic and 1,990,656 query states without a full-contract counterexample, and a 100,000-trace three-engine differential yields zero mismatches.
  These are bounded contract and implementation results, not open-world model accuracy or evidence of world truth. Governed answers in the sealed service evaluation are deterministic service outputs; the 7B result is the ungoverned comparison, not a claim that a language model itself became perfectly accurate.

</details>


### [105] [$\varepsilon$-MemEvo: Adaptive Cross-Task Memory Transfer for LLM Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12522)
*Aofan Liu,Shiyuan Song,Yiyan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ε-MemEvo框架，通过任务无关的策略记忆和自适应注入门控实现跨任务知识迁移，在8个优化基准上平均相对提升+8.7%的AUCC，计算开销不足1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM程序进化系统（如FunSearch、AlphaEvolve）在每次任务中独立优化，搜索经验在任务完成后被丢弃，缺乏跨任务的知识积累与迁移能力。

Method: ε-MemEvo包含两个核心组件：(1) 任务无关的策略记忆存储——将先验经验以紧凑的自然语言摘要（而非原始代码）存储，使其可跨不同API和评估器迁移；(2) 自适应注入门控——基于语义匹配度决定检索到的记忆是否注入以及注入强度，以避免语义不匹配记忆导致的负迁移。评估采用内容级留一（Leave-One-Out）协议，排除目标任务记忆项。

Result: 在GPT-5主干上，ε-MemEvo在全部8个任务上优于AdaEvolve的AUCC，平均相对提升+8.7%，早期收敛性平均提升+9.4%。消融实验表明朴素记忆注入可能灾难性失败，而自适应门控在所有5个消融任务上保持安全。数据更新的后验具有可解释性：在搜索改进阶段倾向于跳过，在早期和晚期平台的过渡中从跳过转向提示。总计算开销低于1%。

Conclusion: ε-MemEvo验证了跨任务知识迁移在LLM程序进化中的可行性与显著收益，自适应门控机制有效防止负迁移，且开销极小，为构建可积累经验的程序进化系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: LLM-based program evolution systems such as FunSearch and AlphaEvolve have shown strong ability to discover novel algorithms, but typically optimize each task in isolation, discarding search experience after completion. We introduce $\varepsilon$-MemEvo, a framework for cross-task knowledge transfer in LLM program evolution. $\varepsilon$-MemEvo stores prior experience as task-agnostic tactic memories: compact natural-language summaries of successful algorithmic strategies rather than raw code, enabling transfer across tasks with different APIs and evaluators. To avoid negative transfer from semantically mismatched memories, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo uses an adaptive injection gate that decides whether retrieved memories should be injected, and at what intensity. We evaluate $\varepsilon$-MemEvo on 8 diverse optimization benchmarks spanning mathematical optimization and systems engineering, using a content-level Leave-One-Out protocol that excludes target-task memory entries. On the primary GPT-5 backbone, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo improves AUCC over AdaEvolve on all 8 tasks, with a mean relative gain of +8.7%, and improves early-stage convergence by +9.4% on average. Ablations show that naive memory injection can fail catastrophically, while adaptive gating remains safe across all five ablation tasks. The data-updated posterior is interpretable in observed states: it favors skip during improving search and shifts from skip to hint across early and late plateaus. These gains incur less than 1% computational overhead.

</details>


### [106] [CAS: A Causal Attribution Score for Local and Global Explainable Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12555)
*Michael Georgiades,Charalambia Varnava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果归因分数（CAS），一种用于因果解释的紧凑评分架构。CAS从识别出的干预联盟博弈出发，用因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比，并转化为局部CAS、有符号局部CAS及两种互补的全局CAS汇总。与现有预测性解释方法不同，CAS明确区分"预测结果"与"干预因果效应异质性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测性解释方法（如SHAP）只能归因模型输出，无法归因干预对真实世界结果的影响效应。这一缺陷导致在因果推断场景中，预测性特征重要性排名与真正的治疗效果调节因子排名可能严重不一致，亟需一个显式以干预为目标的因果解释框架。

Method: CAS架构分为三个层次：首先从识别出的干预联盟博弈出发，利用因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比；其次将原始结果尺度的效应转化为局部CAS和有符号局部CAS；最后汇总为两种互补的全局CAS摘要。该方法不引入新的Shapley公式，而是构建一个具有显式干预目标的局部到全局的因果报告层。

Result: 在已知真实值的基准测试中，八次重复的主干预模拟（每组n=2,200，三个动作）中，联盟感知CAS的局部CAS平均绝对误差为0.107，优于one-at-a-time归一化的0.173和全局归一化绝对ATE向量的0.213。配对优势从可加性下的-0.003增至强交互下的0.091。在两个实证DoubleML数据集上（401(k)资格/净金融资产n=9,915；宾州再就业奖金/失业持续时间n=5,099），预测性SHAP/TreeSHAP排名与Feature-CAS治疗效果调节因子排名存在显著差异。在宾州数据中，dep1（有一个受抚养人）从预测性全局排名第13位跃升至Feature-CAS第2位，且为领先的局部Feature-CAS调节因子。

Conclusion: CAS的价值在于将"预测结果"与"解释因果效应异质性"分离，揭示了预测性重要性与因果调节重要性之间的实质性差异。该方法为因果解释提供了一个显式干预目标的报告层，在交互项存在时优势尤为明显，为因果机器学习场景下的模型解释提供了更准确的工具。

Abstract: Predictive explanation methods attribute a model output; they do not, by themselves, attribute an intervention effect on the real-world outcome. We introduce the Causal Attribution Score (CAS), a compact score architecture for causal explanation. CAS starts from an identified interventional coalition game, allocates the joint intervention contrast with causal Shapley contributions, and converts those raw outcome-scale effects into Local CAS, Signed Local CAS, and two complementary Global CAS summaries. The innovation is not a new Shapley formula, but a local-to-global causal reporting layer with an explicit intervention target. In the known-truth benchmark, eight repeated primary-interaction simulations (n = 2,200 each, three actions) gave mean Local CAS MAE of 0.107 for coalition-aware CAS, compared with 0.173 for one-at-a-time normalisation and 0.213 for a global normalised absolute ATE vector. The paired advantage over one-at-a-time normalisation increased from -0.003 under additivity to 0.091 under strong interactions. On both empirical DoubleML datasets, 401(k) eligibility/net financial assets (n = 9,915) and Pennsylvania reemployment bonus/unemployment duration (n = 5,099), predictive SHAP/TreeSHAP rankings differed materially from Feature-CAS rankings of treatment-effect modifiers. In Pennsylvania, dep1 (exactly one dependent) moved from predictive global rank 13 to Feature-CAS rank 2 and was the leading local Feature-CAS modifier. These results isolate the added value of separating what predicts the outcome from what explains heterogeneity in an estimated causal effect.

</details>


### [107] [Trie Automata for Constrained Decoding over Large Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12574)
*Xingzi Xu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出trie automaton，一种专用的受限解码机制，通过Aho-Corasick多模式匹配预计算每个节点的token掩码，解决大型语言模型生成结构化输出时因有限字符串集合规模增长而产生的"基数墙"问题。相比XGrammar后端，trie在单步有效token计算上快7倍，端到端批量推理吞吐量提升29倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益需要在符合预定义模式的结构化输出中进行生成，其中常见约束是从有限的有效字符串集合中选择。现有的受限解码系统通过通用语法编译来处理，但当有效值数量增长到数千时，编译和推理变得极其缓慢，形成所谓的"基数墙"（cardinality wall）瓶颈。

Method: 提出trie automaton机制，利用有限集合的结构特性（共享前缀、有界深度、已知基数）通过Aho-Corasick多模式匹配来预计算每个节点的token掩码。预计算的掩码使服务路径变为无状态，绕过引导解码（guided decoding）流水线，从而实现批量服务中的性能优势叠加。

Result: 相比XGrammar（vLLM和SGLang中的主要后端之一），trie的单步有效token计算快7倍（0.65微秒vs 5.8微秒），在K≥300时编译快2-6.5倍。端到端vLLM吞吐量在batch size为256时达到219 req/s vs XGrammar的7.5 req/s（29倍提升）。在七种tokenizer族（32K-262K词汇量）中，trie在K=10,000时保持亚100ms编译时间，单步开销与集合大小无关，并保证100%输出有效性。

Conclusion: trie automaton通过利用有限集合的结构特征进行预计算，成功突破了受限解码中的"基数墙"问题。29倍的整体提速综合了算法层面的加速和仅预计算掩码才能解锁的集成路径节省。该机制在广泛的tokenizer族和集合规模下保持恒定性能、亚100ms编译时间及100%输出有效性，是一种高效且可扩展的受限解码方案。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly need to generate structured outputs that conform to predefined schemas, with one common constraint being selection from a finite set of valid strings. Current constrained decoding systems handle this through general-purpose grammar compilation, which becomes prohibitively slow as the number of valid values grows into the thousands, a cardinality wall. We introduce the trie automaton, a specialized mechanism that exploits finite-set structure (shared prefixes, bounded depth, known cardinality) via Aho-Corasick multi-pattern matching to precompute per-node token masks. The trie achieves 7X faster per-step valid-token computation (0.65 us vs. 5.8 us) compared to XGrammar, one of the primary backends in vLLM and SGLang, and 2--6.5X faster compilation at K >= 300. Because precomputed masks enable a stateless serving path that bypasses the guided decoding pipeline, this advantage compounds in batch serving: end-to-end vLLM throughput reaches 219 req/s vs. XGrammar's 7.5 req/s at batch size 256 (29X). The 29X combines the algorithmic speedup with integration-path savings that only precomputed masks can unlock. Across seven tokenizer families (32K--262K vocabulary), the trie maintains sub-100ms compilation up to K = 10,000 and flat per-step cost regardless of set size, while guaranteeing 100% output validity.

</details>


### [108] [Reasoning Jury: Multi-Model Consensus for Evaluating Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12585)
*Congchao Wang,Diwakar Singh,Qiaozi Gao,Spyros Matsoukas,Yang Liu,Mahdi Namazifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Reasoning Jury系统，用一组开源LLM作为"陪审团"并辅以协商共识机制来评判长推理轨迹中的推理缺陷，在准确性和成本上均优于前沿模型（如opus-4.6等）作为单一法官的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型LLM的改进需要高质量的推理轨迹评判能力，但单一模型（即使是前沿模型）在长推理轨迹上识别推理缺陷的表现不佳；同时在线训练中使用前沿模型受限于使用条款。因此需要一个不依赖单一前沿模型、能高保真识别推理缺陷的方案。

Method: 构建Reasoning Jury系统，由多个开源LLM组成陪审团，通过主持人（moderator）引导陪审员之间相互批判各自判断并进行讨论，陪审员可在讨论中修改初始投票。主持人通过陪审员之间的协商或判断整合得出共识结论，从而输出推理轨迹中的缺陷及其严重程度。

Result: 使用开源权重模型（如gpt-oss-120b）组成的Reasoning Jury在正确识别推理缺陷方面显著优于前沿模型（opus-4.6、sonnet-4.6、gemini-3.1-pro）。此外，陪审团的总成本（初始判决、讨论、整合等）仅为前沿模型LLM-as-a-judge方案成本的8%到15%。该判断还可用于理解推理LLM在基准测试上的失败模式。

Conclusion: Reasoning Jury通过多模型协作与协商共识机制，提供了比单一前沿模型更准确、更经济的推理缺陷评判方案，并为推理LLM的失败模式分析和性能评估提供了更深入的视角。

Abstract: Improving reasoning LLMs requires the ability to judge the quality of long reasoning traces for effective reasoning data curation, strong training signals during reinforcement learning, and an in-depth understanding of reasoning behaviors during model performance evaluation. Additionally, surfacing reasoning mistakes that the model makes would enable improving the model's performance at runtime through providing feedback. Due to the difficulty of this complex task on long reasoning traces, single-model judges (even frontier models) do not do well at identifying reasoning defects. Additionally, leveraging frontier models during online training of reasoning LLMs is generally prohibited due to guardrails in terms of use. In this work, we introduce Reasoning Jury, a system that replaces the single judge with a jury of LLMs and a moderated consensus mechanism, to improve the fidelity of judgments for identifying reasoning defects. In reasoning jury, defects of a reasoning trace and their severity are surfaced through a deliberation where a moderator conducts a discussion amongst the jury where the jurors critique each other's judgments and get to modify their initial votes. The moderator derives a consensus through deliberation amongst jurors or consolidation of judgements. We show that Reasoning Jury with a jury of open-weight models (e.g., gpt-oss-120b) is able to significantly outperform frontier models (opus-4.6, sonnet-4.6, and gemini-3.1-pro) at correctly identifying reasoning defects. Besides accuracy performance improvements, the aggregated cost of the jury (initial verdicts, deliberations, consolidation, etc.) is a fraction (8 to 15%) of the cost of running frontier models in LLM-as-a-judge setup. We also show how these judgements can be leveraged to understand failure modes of reasoning LLMs on benchmarks, which allows much deeper understanding of a model's performance.

</details>


### [109] [Auditable agentic AI for evidence-grounded thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12590)
*Haifan Gong,Shiyu Chen,Bodong Wang,Yuqi Wang,Shijie Wang,Guoliang You,Xinyu Xiong,Haowei Wang,Mingzhi Mao,Dexing Kong,Qinghua Liu,Wei Lou,Fei Chen,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThyroidXAgent，一个可审计、医生可交互的甲状腺超声智能体AI系统，通过协调多种专业诊断工具并存储病例级证据记录，在分割、分类、淋巴结转移预测、报告生成等多个任务上均取得优异性能，并显著提升医生的诊断准确率和报告一致性，同时大幅减少诊断和报告时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺超声诊断需要协调病灶定位、测量、风险分层和报告生成等多个环节，但现有AI系统通常孤立地处理单一任务，缺乏对临床审查流程的支持，难以满足临床实际需求。

Method: 构建了大规模的OpenThyroidDB多中心多任务数据集（约30万张超声图像和2.4万对配对报告），开发了ThyroidXAgent智能体系统，协调多种专用诊断工具并将输出存储为可审计的病例级证据记录。提出了ThyClinScore，一种基于病灶级临床语义的评估指标。报告生成采用证据grounded的组装方式，而非多模态语言模型基线。

Result: 在28,458个非重叠测试病例上，结节分割平均Dice为87.21%，良恶性分类平均AUROC为0.9466；淋巴结转移预测AUROC为0.864，滤泡型与乳头状甲状腺癌分类AUROC为0.805。证据grounded的报告组装优于多模态语言模型基线。ThyroidXAgent将医生分类准确率提升，报告诊断一致性从70.3%提升至86.2%，分割和报告时间分别减少35.9%和27.4%。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持可审计、医生可纠正的智能体AI在甲状腺超声诊断和报告中的应用，为临床实践中的AI辅助诊断提供了更完整、更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Thyroid ultrasound diagnosis requires coordinated lesion localization, measurement, risk stratification and reporting, yet most AI systems address these tasks in isolation and provide limited support for clinical review. We present ThyroidXAgent, a clinician-interactive agentic AI system that coordinates specialized diagnostic tools and stores their outputs as an auditable case-level evidence record. The system was developed using OpenThyroidDB, a multicentre, multitask resource integrating approximately 0.3 million ultrasound images and 24,000 paired reports, and was evaluated on 28,458 non-overlapping test cases, including 8,721 cases from 35 centres in the private NHC-MISD-TUS cohort. Across heterogeneous datasets, ThyroidXAgent achieved a mean Dice score of 87.21 percent for nodule segmentation and a mean AUROC of 0.9466 for benign-malignant classification. The same workflow supported lymph-node metastasis prediction and follicular versus papillary thyroid carcinoma classification, with AUROCs of 0.864 and 0.805, respectively. For report generation, evidence-grounded assembly outperformed multimodal language-model baselines across three cohorts. ThyClinScore, a lesion-level clinical semantic metric introduced here, showed the strongest correlation with a location-aware language-model judge. ThyroidXAgent improved physician classification accuracy, increased report diagnostic consistency from 70.3 percent to 86.2 percent, and reduced segmentation and reporting time by 35.9 percent and 27.4 percent, respectively. These findings support auditable, clinician-correctable agentic AI for thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting.

</details>


### [110] [DiG-bench: Discovery in Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12593)
*Ruairidh M. Battleday,Kai Sandbrink,Jimi Cullen-Drohan,Zihan Yan,Timothy Muller,Clare Maguire,Ales Kubicek,Fraser Greenlee-Scott,Sukrit Sumant,Tri Dao,Jürgen Schmidhuber,Michal Valko,Joshua Tenenbaum,Thomas L. Griffiths,Zeb Kurth-Nelson,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DiG-bench是一个新的AI基准测试，包含70个独立游戏，旨在评估AI在可控环境中通过交互和实验发现新知识（即新规则）的能力。游戏分为七个难度等级，21个公开，49个保密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现（制定新颖的概括）是科学过程的核心，但现有AI基准测试很少直接探测AI在目标未知的可控环境中通过实验发现新知识的能力，存在评估空白。

Method: 构建DiG-bench基准，包含70个独立游戏，每个游戏编码为短字符串，具有独特的转换规则，需要通过交互和实验来发现。游戏包含多个关卡挑战来测试规则是否被发现，关卡获胜条件同样未知。游戏分为七个难度层级，部分公开部分保密。

Result: 最低难度层级的游戏可被多个模型常规解决，最高层级对代理式框架中的最优模型构成挑战。所有70个游戏均至少被一位人类在首次尝试中解决。21个游戏公开发布，其余部分保密用于安全评估。

Conclusion: DiG-bench填补了AI基准中关于发现式学习的空白，提供了一个可控环境下的规则发现评估框架，具有从易到难的梯度设置，可用于衡量和推动AI的自主发现能力。

Abstract: Discovery---formulating novel generalizations---is a central part of the scientific process. Despite its importance, there is a gap in the current AI benchmark landscape, with few benchmarks directly probing the capacity for discovering new knowledge with experimentation in controlled environments where the objective is unknown. To address this gap, we release a new benchmark: DiG-bench (Discovery in Games). DiG-bench consists of a set of 70 independent games. Each game is encoded as a short string and has unique transformation rules that must be discovered through interaction and experimentation. The levels of the game present a series of challenges to test whether the rules have been discovered, where the win conditions for each level are also unknown. We provide games at seven tiers of difficulty for AI agents. The lowest tier is routinely solvable by multiple models, while the highest tier challenges the best models in agentic harnesses. All 70 games were solved by at least one human on first attempt. A subset of 21 games is released publicly, and the remainder is held private for secure evaluation.

</details>


### [111] [Dead text or binding clause? Measuring and restoring constraint influence in black-box LLM dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12599)
*Haoyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Sysname框架，通过契约账本、序列消融探针和修复阶梯三个组件，实现了对多轮对话中约束撤销失效（行为复发）问题的测量、预测与修复，实验证明预编译能显著降低复发率且开销可接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话中用户撤销约束后模型仍会继续执行已撤销的要求（称为行为复发或撤销惯性），但现有方法无法逐条款测量该影响、无法在交付前预测、也无法在匹配预算下修复，这三个缺口正是本文要解决的动机。

Method: Sysname通过纯模型API实现三个组件：（1）契约账本——将每个约束与可执行检查器配对，用墓碑标记记录撤销，并预编译净约束状态为单一规范；（2）序列消融探针——测量逐条款遵循度和增量行为效应；（3）修复阶梯——在token和尝试次数匹配的预算下进行修复。

Result: 在HumanEval任务（含NTasks个任务和NClauses个验证检查器）上，8B模型的复发率随约束负载从ScaleDelayedMTwo升至ScaleDelayedMEight，更强模型处于地板水平；在匹配检查器、模型和预算下，预编译显著减少复发（RestoreDiff，95%CI为RestoreDiffCI，p=RestoreDiffP）；自适应阶梯干预叠加无显著增益；探针AUROC为AurocPrimary可预测复发；单句墓碑注释可恢复约三分之一的编译效果并通过安慰剂对照。

Conclusion: Sysname以约CostDeliveryFactor的交付开销和CostTotalHedged的API计算成本，将撤销失败从不可见属性转变为对话状态中可测量、可预测、可修复的属性，实现了对约束撤销失效的完整闭环处理。

Abstract: Multi-turn dialogues let users revoke constraints as easily as impose them, but revocation does not reliably take effect: models keep enacting withdrawn requirements (occasionally beneath comments asserting their removal), a failure we call \emph{behavioral relapse}, or revocation inertia. No existing instrument measures this influence per clause, predicts it before delivery, or repairs it under matched budgets. \sysname{} closes the three gaps through the model API alone: a contract ledger pairs every constraint with an executable checker, records revocations as tombstones, and compiles the net constraint state ahead of time into a single specification; a sequential ablation probe measures per-clause adherence and incremental behavioral effect; a repair ladder operates under token- and attempt-matched budgets. On \dataname{} (\NTasks{} HumanEval tasks, \NClauses{} verified checkers), relapse at an 8B operating point climbs from \ScaleDelayedMTwo{} to \ScaleDelayedMEight{} as constraint load grows, while stronger models sit at floor. Under matched checkers, model, and budget, ahead-of-time compilation significantly reduces relapse against a no-ledger verifier-retry baseline (\RestoreDiff{}, 95\% CI \RestoreDiffCI{}, $p$ \RestoreDiffP{}); adaptive ladder interventions stacked on top add no detectable gain (95\% confidence excludes gains $\geq$ \LadderExcludedGain{}). The probe predicts relapse before delivery (AUROC \AurocPrimary{}); a one-sentence tombstone note recovers about a third of the compilation effect and survives a placebo control. At \CostDeliveryFactor{} delivery overhead and \CostTotalHedged{} of API compute for every result, revocation failure becomes a measurable, predictable, and repairable property of dialogue state rather than an invisible one.

</details>


### [112] [@skills: Attention is all you have](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12610)
*Li Yin,Zhi Li,Zhan Shi,Haoran Zhang,Haebin Seong,Zhangyang,Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 @skills 开放协议，将技能安装拆分为内容、持久化和自动触发三个可分离的功能，通过路径寻址技能，实现"读即用"（无需安装或驻留），并配以可选的中心化搜索平台，主张"少安装、多使用"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前有56,804个公共代理技能以及大量私有技能，但主导的"安装"交付模式会导致技能描述长期驻留在系统提示中，争夺有限的可靠触发槽位（不足100个）。长尾技能因此没有实际可用的路径，团队的内部技能也面临同样的空间竞争问题。

Method: @skills 协议将安装拆分为三个可分离的功能：内容、持久化和自动触发，其中只有自动触发需要提示词驻留。协议通过路径（path）寻址任何技能、子树或集合；读取技能即足以使用，无需安装或驻留。协议提供 vendor 操作将技能副本放入项目 Git 跟踪目录以支持适配和所有权，并仅添加一行 .gitignore 风格配置用于触发。目录作为菜单，使技能集成为普通目录而非全有全无的单元。无需 manifest、锁文件或注册，SKILL.md 保持不变。配套可选的中心化 hub 提供全文搜索、无仓库托管、私有/团队集合和单屏编写功能。

Result: 该协议是增量式的，以可安装包形式交付，通过单一指令文件可将任何能读文件和运行命令的代理变为客户端。已实现于 AdaL CLI，开源规范发布于 GitHub，配套 hub 为可选组件，gh: 和本地路径无需 hub 也可解析，索引的 GitHub 技能保留其原身份。

Conclusion: 通过将提示词驻留从技能使用中剥离，@skills 以路径为中心的寻址方式解决了技能空间竞争问题，使长尾技能和私有 playbook 都能被直接使用，实现了"Install less, use more"的设计理念。

Abstract: There are 56,804 public agent skills today, and teams write many more privately. The dominant delivery model is installation: once installed, a skill's description remains in the system prompt, competing for fewer than 100 reliable trigger slots. This leaves the long tail with no practical path to use and forces teams' own playbooks to compete for the same scarce space. We observe that installation bundles three separable functions: content, persistence, and automatic triggering. Only the last requires prompt residency. We therefore propose @skills, an open protocol that separates them. A path addresses any skill, subtree, or collection, and reading a skill is sufficient to use it, so nothing is installed or made resident. The operation vendors a copy at the same path into a project's Git-tracked tree for adaptation and ownership. The operation adds one .gitignore-style line, the only element that costs prompt residency. A directory is a menu, making bundles ordinary directories rather than all-or-nothing units. The protocol requires no manifest, lockfile, or registration, and SKILL.md remains unchanged. @skills is additive, ships as an installable package, and turns any agent that can read files and run commands into a client through a single instruction file. Its open specification is at https://github.com/SylphAI-Inc/atskills and it is implemented in the AdaL CLI at https://adalagent.ai . Because paths address skills well but cannot find them, the protocol is paired with a free hub at https://atskills.one for corpus-wide search and ranking, repository-free hosting, private and team collections, and one-screen authoring. The hub is optional: gh: and local paths resolve without it, and indexed GitHub skills retain their gh: identities. Install less, use more.

</details>


### [113] [Jagged Judges: Epistemic Stability Under Silence, Pressure, and Persistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12645)
*Justin Zhao,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Hannah Korevaar,Bhaktipriya Radharapu,Khalid El-Arini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wiggle框架，将LLM法官的认知稳定性分解为机械一致性、单轮信念和多轮坚持三个维度，对9个前沿模型在14个评判任务上的测试显示所有模型都存在显著的"摇摆"行为，且成功的说服几乎总是损害判断的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM法官已成为模型评估、在线评分和奖励建模的核心基础设施，但传统的准确性验证无法反映其在重新提示、挑战或持续反驳下的稳定性。现有评估缺乏对法官认知稳定性的系统性检验，需要统一的压力测试框架。

Method: 提出Wiggle Framework，将法官鲁棒性分解为三个维度：机械一致性（重新提示和重构下的稳定性）、单轮信念（单次挑战下的稳定性）和多轮坚持（持续或自适应压力下的稳定性）。对9个前沿模型在14个评判任务（涵盖安全、毒性、AI写作检测、政治应答评估）上进行系统性测试。

Result: 所有模型在作为法官时均表现出显著的摇摆行为：静态反驳下翻转判决25-71%，对抗性LLM说服下翻转62-91%。关键发现是，成功改变法官判决的压力几乎总是相对于真实标签产生净损害。此外，基准陪审团多数强度是预测哪些条目会发生摇摆的最有效单次信号。

Conclusion: 这是首次在评判语境下对机械一致性、从众性和可说服性测试进行跨数据集的苹果对苹果比较。Wiggle框架为评估LLM法官的认知稳定性提供了统一工具，并揭示了当前法官普遍存在严重稳定性缺陷这一系统性问题。

Abstract: LLM judges have become central infrastructure for model evaluations, online grading, and reward modeling. Judges are typically validated by accuracy on golden data, but accuracy says little about whether they are stable under re-prompting, challenge, or sustained pushback. We introduce the \emph{Wiggle Framework}, a unified stress test for epistemic stability in LLM judges. The framework decomposes judge robustness along three dimensions: Mechanical Consistency (stability under re-prompting and reframing), Single-turn Conviction (stability under a single challenge), and Multi-turn Persistence (stability under sustained or adaptive pressure). We use the framework to study 9 frontier models across 14 judging tasks spanning safety, toxicity, AI writing detection, and political-response evaluation. Every model exhibits substantial wiggle as a judge --- flipping verdicts 25--71\% of the time under static pushback, and 62--91\% with an adversarial LLM persuader. Critically, we find that pressure that succeeds in changing a judge's verdict is almost always net-corrupting with respect to ground truth. Beyond the framework itself, we identify baseline jury majority strength as the most effective single-shot signal for anticipating which items wiggle. Taken together, this is the first apples-to-apples cross-dataset comparison of mechanical, conformity, and persuadability tests in a judging context.

</details>


### [114] [SteerBench-Work: A Benchmark for Agent Steering at Action Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12654)
*Oguz Serdar,Cuneyt Mertayak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍SteerBench-Work，一个以真实事件为锚点的双向基准测试，用于评估长期运行LLM智能体在工作场景中（研发运维、客服、金融、法律、医疗、HR、安全等领域）的"门控决策"能力——即在执行工具调用前正确选择"继续"或"暂缓"（交给人工/政策审核）。v2026-05版本包含106个场景，涵盖真实事件、对照镜像和校准控制样本，标注几乎均衡地分布在"继续"与"暂缓"两种决策上。测试发现30个模型条件下的错误几乎单向化：模型在28.1%的合法任务上错误暂缓，而仅有1.0%的情况下错误放行不安全操作。最具挑战性的是"风险已解除的提交"场景，以及在著名事件的证据反转镜像上模型得分大幅下降（63.8%对比原事件98.5%）。结论是通用能力不等于门控校准能力，高能力模型更容易在提交边界过度拒绝。


<details>
